Posts for 2016-08

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Aug 01 2016 06:44:20 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

(1) JurySys was NOT ended due to Nanavati case --- Mr. Jawaharlal ended Jury System in 1959 to protect landlords' large land holding. All in all, the Jurors across India were siding with landless and so ending Jury System had become must to protect landlords !!! And so Mr. Jawaharlal wanted to end Jury System and took Nanavati case as excuse.
.
(2) Jurors decided to "acquit" Nanavati, NOT because of media campaign . But because Jurors saw Ahuja as "paternity fraudster criminal", and saw Captain Kavas Nanavati as potential victim of "paternity fraud" or "paternity uncertainty". And Jurors acquitted Nanavati because Jurors were NOT given option of awarding say 3 years in prison. The only option given to Jurors was "acquit" or "life in prison" and so they decided "acquit".
.
(3) The movie Rustom gives wrong information. It repeats the lie that "Jury System was ended due Nanavati case". Whereas in reality, Mr. Jawaherlal etc ended Jury System only to protect landlords.
.
======
.
This post addresses two separate issue. One is Nanavati case. And second why Jawaharlal etc ended JurySys India.
.
======
.
(1) JurySys was NOT ended due to Nanavati case --- it was ended by Mr. Jawaharlal because Jurors across India were siding with landless, and giving verdict against landlords's criminals
.
Mr. Jawaherlal had printed a Gazette Notification in 1959 to end Jury System in India, and that GN became effective in courts after it was signed by the then Supreme Court judges.
.
Mr. Jawaherlal has cited Nanavati case as an excuse !!! But then did Mr. Jawaharlal always speak the truth? And did he always give right statements on motives behind his actions on all matters? And if decision of Jury to acquit Nanavati was wrong, and if Nanavati deserved to die, then why did Jawaherlal himself ordered his sister and Mahashrashtra Governor Vijaya Laxmi Pandti to hive Gubernatorial Pardon to Nanavati later?
.
We know that Mr. Jawaherlal had lied on many many occasions. Then it is quite possible that lied on this issue too --- that he had some other motives to ban Jury System, and he used Nanavati case ONLY as an excuse.
.
So only evidence to explain motives to Mr. Jawaherlal is his statement, And we know that M,. Jawaherlal had lied 10s and 100s of times. So one is free is to say "yes, Jawaherlal did lie on many occasions, but on this issue , that "reason why he ended Jury System was Nanavati case", he was speaking all truth. But there are many who believed that Jawaherlal had some other motives in ending Jury System. What could have been his motives?

Now lets make one statistical observation. Today, there are some 3 crore pending cases in some 18000 Lower Courts across India. And every year, some 5 lakh to 10 lakh new cases get filed, To this, add cases in 100s of tribunals. And in 1959, some one lack new cases were getting filed every year.
.
So a question in 1959 would have been "should we use Jury System in these lakhs of pending cases and 10000s new cases that come every year, or shall we use judge system?"
.
Now , IMO, it is NOT possible to decide this question, based on outcome of just one case namely Nanavati case.
.
The whole statement , that "one case, namely Nanavati case, was real reason that decided the question "should we use Jury System or judge in lakhs and lakhs of court cases" is farce. No sane and honest human will every ever use just one case to decide the system that needs to be used to give judgments in lakhs and lakhs of cases. Jawaharlal had high "cunning IQ" but low "statistical IQ" and low "mathematical IQ". So Mr. Jawaharlal was cunning but had no constructive intelligence was mentally incapable of taking decision that required statistical analysis. But even with such low IQ, he could have seen that one Nanavati case can never a good reason to decide what system should be used in to decide 1000s of cases.
.
So if Nanavati case was not sufficient reason reason, or not even real reason to end Jury System in India, what other motives Jawaherlal had in his minds?
.
Lets examine the land reforms issue which was central to politics from 1950 to 1980 and is critical issue even today.
.
Back in 1950s, over 85% to 90% Indians lived in villages. And by villages , I mean real villages --- no electricity, no roads, no runni water, no sewers, no hospitals etc. And less than 3% families owned over 90% of land in India, exceptstates of Gujarat, JK, Kerala , Punjab and Haryana.
.
In UP, Bihar etc, landlords owned miles and miles of land. And landless used to be real poor --- real poor --- starving poor. A poverty that we dont see today.
.
The landlords used to pay pittance to the landless laborers. And in addition, atrocities, kidnapping, beatings, insulting etc were norms. Many landlords were courts and police by themselves,
.
All party workers, such as jansangh / CPM / Congress workers wanted a solution to the problem of poverty of the landless and recklessness of the landlords. Some CPM middle level workers started demanding land reforms -- i.e. take away land or landlords and ex-kings and give it to landless. Most CPM leaders , all congress leaders and all jansangh apex leaders opposed it. But proposal became too too popular in workers across parties.
.
So in short --- the concept that landlords' land should be acquired by state and given to landless --- aka land reforms -- had become too too popular in workers across Jansangh, RSS,
.
So Jawaharlal and other MPs were forced to print law that if a landlord owns over 150 acre of land, then he can keep any 150 acre and rest will be given to tillers !!
..
So the tillers started going to court with claims. And so landlords started using criminals to beat the landless. But the landlords could not get far, because Jurors would imprison criminals and so criminals were not wiling to beat the landless anymore.
.
So ending Jury System had become necessary to ensure that landless can use criminals to beat the landless and stop them from going to courts. And so Jawaharlal ended Jury System in India.
.
Now why did 100s of MPs etc helped Jawaharlal on this issue? Well, most MPs were from landlord families and they didnt really want and upliftment of landless. So they too wanted to end Jury System in India. And so they all supported Jawaharlal on the issue of ending Jury System in India.
.
=====
.
(2) Why did Jurors freed Nanavati ?
.
(2a) Understanding the problem of paternity uncertainty, as it was decades ago ---
.
IMO, understanding the issue of paternity uncertainty is central to Nanavati case and 1000s of cases involving adultery and also understanding, why today society doesnt consider adultery as heinous crime, and in past it used to consider adultery as .a heinous crime.
.
Today, science and engineering, and medicine, has solved a problem that had plagued mankind across world for over 20000 years --- paternity uncertainty/fraud and avoiding unwanted pregnancy !!! Thanks to blood tests and DNA test, the problem of paternity uncertainty and paternity fraud has now become zero. And thanks to pills etc, problem of unwanted pregnancy also no longer exists.
.
Today, fathers and mothers impose less restriction on daughters than say 70 years ago or 1000 years ago. And brothers impose less restriction on sisters and husband impose less restrictions on wives and so on. And the main reason is NOT some novels written by feminists and assorted sociologists. The real and ONLY reasons are two -- hard work of scientists, doctors and engineers have ensured that (a) unwanted pregnancies wont happen.(b) blood tests and DNA tests have reduced paternity uncertainty to zero
.
For time immemorial, males and females (like mothers) had imposed restriction on young females, and reason was --- what if freedom results into non marital sex, and that non marital sex happens in pregnancy, and husband has no way to know whether he is the real father of the child or someone else. Whole life, father will have to live with suspicion and agony that child he is raising may not be his own. And adultery also creates ground for someone who gets sadistic enjoyment in committing "paternity fraud" !! All this statement may appear as useless today, because the crime has vanished due to blood tests and DNA tests, and that women can avoid / end unwanted pregnancy with no risk. But I would request readers to imagine situation some 70 years back when there were no effective blood tests and DNA test was unheard --- in fact DNA itself was not even discovered !! In many ways, rape is worse than murder, and betrayal is worse than rape, and paternity fraud is worse than betrayal because victim has no way to know that he is victim !! Of course, now this crime doesnt happen --- thanks to science.
..
The society too back then had to worry about unwanted children to unmarried women or married women. Because back then, social security system was not robust, and such out of wedlock children were more likely to become violent criminals. Today, society doesnt have MATERIAL reason worry about non-marital sex. Because pre-marital sex will NOT result in unwanted pregancy. And so physical danger from a out of wedlock child are very low.
.
Also, back then society had to worry about agony a man can suffer if he doubts that the child isnt his own, and there is no scientific way to prove or disprove it.
.
And so back then, across world, society consider adultery as a crime worse than murder, It was NOT because society considered woman as property of man, These nonsenses, that "men consider women as their property": make good novel lines and sociology essays, but are useless. The MATERIAL reason why men and women across globe considered adultery as heinous crime was paternity uncertainty it creates and opportunity it creates for a criminal to create paternity fraud. And as science solved the problem of paternity uncertainty, the adultery almost became non-crime.
..
So while analyzing Jurors' decision in Nanavati case, I request readers to keep the concept of paternity uncertainty in mind. Today, you, the reader, may or may not consider paternity as a crime. But back then, over 90% population in India, and even in Western countries, consider paternity fraud as heinous crime. And so they considered adultery as a crime.
.
-----
.
(2b) When victim in round-two was heinous criminal in first round, and the criminal of second round had been victim in first round !!!
.
If I ask you, "what punishment you give to a murderer", you may say "14 years in prison or 20 years in prison or life in prison or execution".
.
But let me ask following question
.
(i) A 60 years old man X decided to put all his life savings of say Rs 1 crore in buying two flats --- he planed to live in once and rent the other.
.
(ii) He bought two flats from a builder B
.
(iii) Later, buyer X found that builder had already sold those tow flats to someone else, and B has cheated him, and he lost his life's savings
.
(iv) X takes a gun, and X asks B to repay the money. B refuses and X shows gun to him, and in the fight, X kills B.
.
(v) say later investigation show that B was habitual criminal and B had duped many in past and B had also planned to dupe many in future
.
Now X is criminal. But thats in round-2. In round-1, builder B was criminal and that too a habitual criminal. And he had planned to duped many in future. In away, X saved many future victims from becoming victims.
.
Also, it is clear that murderer X would have never committed murder if he was not duped to such an extent. And murderer X had never committed any crime in past.
.
So this crime is case where ":the criminal in round-2 had been victim in round-1 and victim of round-2 had been a criminal, and that too habitual deliberate criminal, in round-1".
.
Now lets say we ask 10000 persons chosen at random "what punishment would you give on X for murder of B --- (a) zero (b) 3 years in prison (c) 14 years in prison (d) 20 years in prison or (e) execution".
.
I cant speak for all 10000. But if I am Jury, my answer will be somewhere between (a) and (b). In fact, I may give (a) and say "than you for saving many future victims from becoming victims of B".
,
IOW, when criminal in round-2 had been victim in round-1, and victim of round-2 had been criminal in round-1, then there is a tendency NOT to give full punishment and give "discount in punishment".
.
And when victim of round-2 has been career criminal in round-1 who had duped many, the hatred for victim increases discount that criminal may get. And the possibility that that victim would would have duped many more, further increases tendency to say "thank you" and give more discount in the punishment.
.
And in case of paternity fraud, and aduletry cases 70 years back, the case was worse than cheating I mentioned above. Because there was no way to prove / disprove paternity
,
And in way, this discount in punishment is what keeps criminals in check. Because if every criminal is told that "commit a crime, and you will be punished ONLY if proved and there will be zero private consequences if you commit a crime in unprovable way", then crime rate will jump. Because in many cases, wanna be criminals deter from possible private action and less from courts. IOW, giving discount to criminal of second round is one way to say "thank you for saving future victim".
.
So I request you keep his factor in mind. That many persons across the world have this notion that "when criminal in round-2 had been victim in round-1, and victim of round-2 had been criminal in round-1, then there is a tendency NOT to give full punishment and give some discount in punishment".

----
.
(2c) To acquit or imprison for 3 years or life imprisonment or execution?
.
So when Jurors saw the case, 70 years back, they saw it as follows
.
(2c.1) Nanavati was not a habitual criminal
.
(2c.2) Ahuja had option of commercial sex , which back then was 100% legal. But he decided to do adultery, knowing that it can cause paternity uncertainty and cause grave psychological and physical problems on Nanavati and the child, had a child been born out of Ahuja's relation with Nanavati's wife. IOW, Ahuja was a reckless criminal
.
(2c.3) Ahuja had indulged into adultery in past, and logically planned to do it again several times.
.
(2c.4) So Nanavati didnt deserve life sentence or execution, or even a long sentence. At worst, he deserved say 3 years to 4 years in prison.

------
.
(2d) Jurors were given only two choices --- not guilty or judge will decide "life imprisonment or execution".
.
The system in Nanavati case did not give power to Jurors to decide length of punishment,. The Jurors could have said "not guilty" and if they had said "guilty" then judges would have decided the punishment --- be life sentence or execution..
.
The Jurors did NOT have option to say "three years in prison". or "five years in prison"/
.
So Jurors effectively had only two choices - (a) zero punishment OR (b) let judge decide execution or life sentence
.
And execution in this case, where so victim Ahuja was a criminal in first round, and criminal Nanavati was a victim in first round , was perhaps unacceptable to them. And so was life sentence.
.
Basically, Jurors did NOT wish to say "life sentence or execution" . And so they had only once choice --- say "not guilty".
.
IOW, the Jurors only picked the option which they thought was lesser of the two bad options offered to them. If they had choice of "three years in prison", then they may have gone for it.
.
-----------------------
.
(2e) All in all, IMO the Jurors in Nanavati case could or should have said "three years in prison", but they didnt have that option. They had two choices (i) zero punishment OR (ii) let judge pick life imprisonment or execution. And given the gravity of paternity uncertainity and gravity of adultery 70 years back, Jurors ruled out (b). And so only choice they had was (a).
.
Today, evaluating the decision of Jurors in Nanavati case is difficult because today, crime of paternity uncertainty and unwanted pregnancy is no more thanks birth control and DNA tests. So today, one may wrongly say "Jurors were not rational and were emotional" etc. But Jurors did apply full mind, and took a rational decision amongst the choices they had
.
======
.
(3) Movie Rustom is wrong
.
Well, when was a Bollywood movie right? Movie is movie --- it need not be right.
.
But Rustom movie propagates same lie -- that the then PM Jawaherlal ended Jury System due to Nanavati case, Well, Nanavati could have been an excuse at best, Otherwise, outcome of one case will not effect decision of any person on "system for lakhs of case" question. Nanavati case was only an excuse. Jawaherlal wanted to end Jury System to protect landlords, because Juries were siding with landless.
.
To know more on Jury System, pls see chap-21 and chap-22 of
Right to Recall Manifesto, English, aka 301.pdf at https://www.facebook.com/groups/righttorecallparty/10152114637883103/ and Right to Recall Manifesto, Hindi, aka 301.h.pdf at https://www.facebook.com/groups/righttorecallparty/10152114645373103/ .

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Aug 01 2016 06:58:26 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

The issue of hospitals charging MRP and not the actual price of the medcine
.
Say a hospital get medicine for say Rs 100. Say MRP is Rs 1000
.
Now hospital has two choices
.
1) hospital can say -
medicine price = Rs 100
hospital fees = Rs 900
Total = Rs 1000, plus applicable taxes
,
2) hospital can say
medicine price = Rs 800 (a lie, because hosiptal got for Rs 100)
hospital fees = Rs 200
Total = Rs 1000, plus taxes
.
In first case, hospital is honest. But service tax it will have to pay is 15% of administration charges will be Rs 135 . So total bill on patient will be Rs 1135
.
In second case, hospital is speaking lie. But service tax will be Rs 30. and so total charge on on patient will be Rs 1030
.
Thats why hospitals show higher medicine price and lesser service change. Because there is service tax on hospital fees
.
SOLUTION?
.
Remove service tax, impose wealth tax minus income tax paid minus credits minus 15% of salaries paid to staff. Now hospitals may get credits from patients and hospitals generally pay good salary to staff. So effective WT on hospitals will be low or even zero. But wealth tax on those who have empty prime location plots will be huge. This will force them to rent / sell prime land, and so land prices will reduce, and so price of ALL things including hospital fees will fall.
.
And the solution to high medical costs is to improve medicine education --- Right to recall health Minister , and improve medicine in govt hospitals.
.
Now , you all may note that paidjournalists etc who "expose" hospitals insist on silence on land price issue. Why?Well, see how much land paid-ToI's owners own.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Mon Aug 01 2016 16:58:31 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

The solution to "missing trader problem" of GST / VAT
.
There are several status posts of mine which describe missing trader problem in GST / VAT. Please search in
.http://mehtarcfbcopy.blogspot.in/2016/02/dear-recallists-please-do-google-on.html . Please also see my video on VAT / GST at youtube.com/RightToRecallGroup
.
===
.
The solution is as follows
.
(1) A trader named A who is giving tax invoice will need to have two accounts - (1a) normal account of A , like today (1b) GST account of A. IOW, banks will provide two types of account.
.
(2) Normal accounts are like accounts of today
.
(3) GST accounts has following restrictions
.
(3a) Money deposited in GST account can be given to govt as GST payment OR transferred to GST account of seller, and thats all,. i.e. money from GST account cannot be moved into normal account.
.
(3b) one can transfer money from normal account to GST account, but not vice versa. If one wants money back from his GST account, then he will need to first pay govt, and then claim refund.
.
====
.
How does this solve missing trader problem?
.
Say A sells goods to B at say Rs 1000 and GST rate is say 20%
.
The B should write two cheques
.
(a)Rs 1000 in name of A, from his normal account
(b)Rs 200 in name of "GST account , A", from his GST account
.
cheque (b) will also have invoice number as additional detail
.
So now if A doesnt deposit second cheque, then B has that Rs 200 in his GST account which he can later pay to govt. And if A deposits the second cheque of he got from B, then from A' GST account, A can only pay GST or pay to govt but he cannot transfer money to his personal account or anyone else's personal account. IOW, the GST paid will never get siphoned out.
.
THUS, GST-account-scheme solves missing trader problem.
.
This works only when payment is made via cheque.
.
If one is paying via cash, then also it can be implemented. I will explain that in tomorow's status.
.
This doesnt need any change in tax law that calculate next tax due. The govt only need to order RBI to order banks to create a new type of account.
.
====
.
Reducing "double taxation"
----------------------------------
.
Now this scheme of GST account can be extended to reduce double taxation i.e. one will have to pay income tax or GST but no both !! How?
.
Say a buyer buys goods for say Rs 1000 + Rs 200 GST = Rs 1200 , and pays via card Then the merchant will make two entries Rs 1000 goods and Rs 200 GST. And the credit card statement will have two columns as well. The credit card will also show GST paid between 1-april and 31-march at year end
.
So whats the use?
.
The GST paid on record can be made rebate in income tax !!!
.
So this proposal can benefit everyone who is dealing in white money. But now he has to pay income tax or GST but not both.
.
So say a person has earned Rs 20,00,000 and say income tax due is Rs 500,000. Now say during the year he has recorded payment of GST as Rs 100,000. Then income tax he will have to pay will be only Rs 400,000
.
===
.
Both proposals --- GST-account-scheme and deducting GST paid from income tax due are SEPARATE proposal.
.
We at Right to recall party should work to ask activists to ask traders to order MPs via SMS to implement GST-account-scheme and give GST paid as rebate in income tax.
.
Both will be two separate SMS.
.
I will put drafts in 1-2 months
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Tue Aug 02 2016 01:47:41 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

In jun-2011 ,when Janlokpal Movement was at its peak, I had taken interview of the then chairman WalleMarte chairman Lee Scoute. The interview was in English. This is modification Hindi translation. Hindi translation.was done by Suheldev Pathak)

Please note --- the interview was taken in jun-2011.
.
The main issue was --- Anna and Chhote Anne (namely ArKe, KiBe etc) were all opposing the proposal to add Right to recall Janlokpal clauses in their proposed Janlokpal bill. To know why, I decided to interview the chief sponsor of janlokpal, the WallMarte chairman himself.
.
=====.
RM - आपका धन्यवाद जो आपने कीमती समय दिया !
.
Lee Scott - मेरा सौभाग्य , हम मजबूत लोकपाल जल्दी से जल्दी लाना चाहते है। साक्षात्कार लेने के लये धन्यवाद !
.
---
.
RM - सबसे पहले आप लोकपाल को समर्थन क्यों कर रहे है ?लोग कहते है बहुराष्ट्रीय कंपनिया पैसे खिलाती है , क्या लोकपाल आने से आपके व्यापारिक हितो को चोट नहीं लगेगी ?
.
Lee Scott - एक बार लोकपाल आ गया तो हमे सिर्फ 11 लोकपाल को रिश्वत देनी होगी। और इस तरह हम 25000 अधिकारियो और मंत्रियो को नियंत्रित कर पाएंगे।
.
-----
.
RM - जरा समझाइये ?
.
Lee Scott - आज हमे 25000 अधिकारीयों - 2000 कलेक्टर स्तर के आईएएस , 2000 लेवल आईपीएस , 2000 डिस्ट्रिक्ट प्रोसेक्यूटर्स , 10000 डिस्ट्रिक्ट जज , 5000 MLA , 800 MP , 700 राज्य स्तर मंत्री , 700 हाई कोर्ट जज , आदि आदि को रिश्वत देनी पढ़ती है। में यह सब अमेरिका में बैठ के नहीं कर सकता। और मुझे राडिया जैसी लॉबिस्ट को 200 % मार्क उप चार्ज देना पढता है। पर एक बार लोकपाल आ गया तो 11 लोकपाल को रिश्वत देके 25000 अधिकारियो और मंत्रियो को नीयंत्रित कर सकता हूँ।
.
---
.
RM - आप उदाहरण दे सकते है ?
.
Lee Scott - रिटेल में FDI विरोध की बात देखे। मुझे 800 सांसदों को रिश्वत देनी होगी उनके समर्थन के लिए। अब में 11 लोकपाल को रिश्वत देके उनके द्वारा 800 सांसदों को मजबूर कर सकता हूँ समर्थन के लिए। लोकपाल उन सांसदों के खिलाफ शिकायत दर्ज करेगा जो खुदरा में FDI का विरोध करेगा और मीडिया में मेरे एजेंट उन सांसदों को भ्रष्ट बता देगा। 2 संसद अंदर जायेंगे तो 800 अपने आप पालन करेंगे मेरा।
..
---
.
RM - तो आपका कहना लोकपाल भ्रष्टाचार खत्म नहीं करेगा ?
.
Lee Scott - नहीं भ्रष्टाचार काम होगा , पहले जो हम ज्यादा रिश्वत देनी पड़ती थी ,अब हमे कम देनी होगी कोई काम करवाने को। उदाहरण - पहले मुझे 800 सांसदों के लिए 3000 करोड़ देना होगा अब सिर्फ 100 करोड़ में काम हो जायेगा। तो भ्रष्टाचार कम हुआ के नहीं।
.
---
.
RM -पर अन्ना कहते है जो लोकपाल रिश्वत लेगा उसे सुप्रीम कोर्ट के जज नौकरी से निकल देंगे ?
.
Lee Scott - में इतना बेवकूफ नहीं जो लोकपाल को कैमरा के सामने रिश्वत दूंगा। न ही लोकपाल जनता के बीच पैसे लेंगे। में या तो स्विस बैंक या मॉरिशस बैंक में पैसे जमा करवाऊंगा या उसके किसी रिश्तेदार को सलाहकार या वकील के तोर पे रखूँगा और उसको फीस के तोर पे रकम चुकाऊंगा।
.
हमने हजारो बार रिश्तोदारो को ऐसे ही रिश्वत खिलाई है भारत और बहोत से देशो में। हमारे पास सुप्रीम कोर्ट के जजो के बहोत से रिश्तेदार हमारे यहाँ वकील है। वे बहोत सक्षम है और धायण रखेंगे की हमारे एजेंट लोकपाल को कोई नुकसान न हो। ड्रामा खड़ा करने के लिए हम १-२ लोकपाल को फसा के निष्काषित करवा देंगे ताकि नागरिको की आस्था लोकपाल में बानी रहे।
.
---
.
RM - लेकिन लोगो को पता तो चल ही जायेगा ?
.
Lee Scott - पता चल भी जायेगा तो क्या। राइट टू रिकॉल तो है नहीं। जैसे भ्रस्ट मंत्री 5 साल नहीं हटते यह भी नहीं हटेंगे।
.
---
.
RM - तो आप भारत में जनलोकपाल लाने के लिए क्या कदम उठा रहे है ?
.
Lee Scott - मेने और बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियो के मालिको ने प्रचार अभियान के लिए 10000 करोड़ खर्च करने का फैसला लिया है। हम इक्विटी या लोन या विज्ञापन के नाम पर टीवी चैनेलो को पैसे देंगे जो अन्ना और लोकपाल को पॉजिटिव में दिखाए और इनका विरोध अकरने वालो को नेगेटिव में। हम, महात्मा अन्ना और लोकपाल को दिन के 48 घण्टे , सप्ताह के 14 दिन और साल के 100 सप्ताह दिखने के लिए पैसे देंगे। यहाँ तक की सास बहु सीरियल में भी अन्ना और लोकपाल नामसे टिकर चलेगा । टीवी चैनेलो का इस्तेमाल करके हम अन्ना को भगवन बना देंगे।
.
---
.
RM - क्या आप राइट तो रिकॉल लोकपाल का समर्थन करते है ?
.
Lee Scott - ( गुस्से में और चिल्ला के ) कभी नहीं। फिर हमे 35 करोड़ नागरिको को रिश्वत देनी होगी। हम 100,200 या 5000 लोगो को रिश्वत खिला सकते है 35 करोड़ लोगो को नहीं। हम कभी नहीं चाहेंगे राइट तो रिकॉल लोकपाल का खंड लोकपाल ड्राफ्ट में आये।
.
---
.
RM - क्या होगा अगर अन्ना राइट तो रिकॉल लोकपाल का समर्थन करे।
.
Lee Scott - तो में मीडिया में उसकी कवरेज के लिए पैसे देना बांध कर दूंगा और मीडिया को बता दूंगा कभी राइट तो रिकॉल जैसा शब्द लोगो न पता चले। और मुझे नहीं लगता महात्मा अन्ना इसे स्वीकार करेंगे। उन्हें शोहरत चाहिए टीवी पर जो वो कभी खोना नहीं चाहेंगे।
.
---
.
RM - क्या आप अन्ना और उनके लोगो को रिश्वत खिला रहे है ?
.

Lee Scott - नहीं। महात्मा अन्ना ने कभी पैसे नहीं मांगे और न ही मांगेंगे। वो 100 % ईमानदार है जब बात पैसे की हो। उनके सभी आदमी ईमानदार है। महात्मा अन्ना और उनके आदमियो को शोहरत चाहिए। महात्मा अन्ना को इतिहास की पुस्तको में अपना नाम चाहिए, नोबल प्राइज चाहिए। नेहरू और महत्मा गांधी जैसे बहोत से लोग है जो शोहरत के लिए देश हितो का बलिदान दे देंगे। हम टीवी चैनेलो को पैसा खिला के यकीन दिलवा देंगे महत्मा अन्ना भगवन है बदले में अन्ना हमे बिना राइट टू रिकॉल वाला जनलोकपाल लेन में मदद कर्नेगे !
.
---
.
RM - तो अन्ना आपके एजेंट है ?
.
Lee Scott - एजेंट गलत शब्द होगा। महात्मा अन्ना हमारे एजेंट कभी नहीं बनेगे। वह हमारे सहजीवी साथी है - हम लोकपाल के अल्पाधिकार चाहते है वो प्रसिद्धि। हम उनकी मदद करेंगे वो हमारी मदद राइट टू रिकॉल के बिना लोकपाल लेन में करेंगे।
.
---
.
RM -आपको लोकपाल ही चाहिए तो जंतर मंतर पर अनशन करने का विचार आपके मन में क्यों नहीं आया ?
.
Lee Scott - में खुश हूँ आपने यह प्रशन पूछा। असल में मैं तयारी कर रहा था। टीवी चैनेलो को 2000 करोड़ रुप्पय्ये के भुगतान का विचार बना रहा था ताकि वे मेरे लिए भावनात्मक लहर पैदा करे। तुम्हे पता है में $ 50,000 का अरमानी सूट , $ 10,000 की टाई , $ 5000 का शर्ट , $ 20.000 के गुसी के जूते , पहनता हूँ। पर अनशन के लिए मेने $2000 का अरमानी सूट , सिर्फ $10 की टाई , $50 का शर्ट , और $500के जूते ख़रीदे। पर मेरे सलाहकारों ने कहा आप 1000 बार अनशन अकरके मर जायेंगे पर को भारतीय अप्पकी आवाज नहीं सुनेगा। इसके लिए आपको ऐसे व्यक्ति की जरुरत है जो भारतीयों को रुला सके। व्यक्ति साधारण कपडे पहनता हो , आमिर नहीं होना चाहिए , बुजुर्ग हो , और उसका अतीत अछा हो। तो मेने अपना फैसला बदला और मीडिया को 2000 करोड़ रूपपए दिए महत्मा अन्ना को बताने के लिए। जिन रामदेव पर रत 2 बजे लाठिया बरसी मेने अन्ना को सुबह 15 अगस्त को 8 बजे गिरफ्तार करवाया . जब पूरा देश टीवी देख रहा हो।
.
---
.
RM - appka धन्यवाद ली स्कॉट।
.
ली स्कॉट - welcome , और धन्यवाद भारतीय नागरिको को यह बताने के लिए बिना राइट टू रिकॉल के जनलोकपाल कितना खतरनाक साबित होगा।
.नोट - : यह इंटरव्यू काल्पनिक है। यह इस सन्दर्भ में दिया गया है की लोकपाल का ड्रामा कार्यकर्ता या नागरिक आसानी से समझ सके।
.
---
.
राइट टू रिकॉल द्वारा सुझाये गए राइट टू रिकॉल कानून यहाँ से डाउनलोड करे
.
मुफ्त डाउनलोड लिंक – http://www.righttorecall.info/301.h.pdf
और http://www.righttorecall.info/301.h.doc
.

.

Pawan Jury BhilwaraRj

Tue Aug 02 2016 03:36:15 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

सुहेल देव पाठक:

नोट - ये एक व्यंग्य है

वॉलमार्ट चैयरमेन, ली स्कॉट का इंटरव्यू राइट टू रिकॉल ग्रुप के साथ !क्यों वो जनलोकपाल का समर्थन कर रहे है ?
हमारी तरफ से इंटरव्यू लेंगे राहुल मेहता (RM)
==============
.
RM - आपका धन्यवाद जो आपने कीमती समय दिया !
.
Lee Scott - मेरा सौभाग्य , हम मजबूत लोकपाल जल्दी से जल्दी लाना चाहते है। साक्षात्कार लेने के लये धन्यवाद !
.
=============
.
RM - सबसे पहले आप लोकपाल को समर्थन क्यों कर रहे है ?लोग कहते है बहुराष्ट्रीय कंपनिया पैसे खिलाती है , क्या लोकपाल आने से आपके व्यापारिक हितो को चोट नहीं लगेगी ?
.
Lee Scott - एक बार लोकपाल आ गया तो हमे सिर्फ 11 लोकपाल को रिश्वत देनी होगी। और इस तरह हम 25000 अधिकारियो और मंत्रियो को नियंत्रित कर पाएंगे।
==============
.
RM - जरा समझाइये ?
.
Lee Scott - आज हमे 25000 अधिकारीयों - 2000 कलेक्टर स्तर के आईएएस , 2000 लेवल आईपीएस , 2000 डिस्ट्रिक्ट प्रोसेक्यूटर्स , 10000 डिस्ट्रिक्ट जज , 5000 MLA , 800 MP , 700 राज्य स्तर मंत्री , 700 हाई कोर्ट जज , आदि आदि को रिश्वत देनी पढ़ती है। में यह सब अमेरिका में बैठ के नहीं कर सकता। और मुझे राडिया जैसी लॉबिस्ट को 200 % मार्क उप चार्ज देना पढता है। पर एक बार लोकपाल आ गया तो 11 लोकपाल को रिश्वत देके 25000 अधिकारियो और मंत्रियो को नीयंत्रित कर सकता हूँ।
.
=================
.
RM - आप उदाहरण दे सकते है ?
.
Lee Scott - रिटेल में FDI विरोध की बात देखे। मुझे 800 सांसदों को रिश्वत देनी होगी उनके समर्थन के लिए। अब में 11 लोकपाल को रिश्वत देके उनके द्वारा 800 सांसदों को मजबूर कर सकता हूँ समर्थन के लिए। लोकपाल उन सांसदों के खिलाफ शिकायत दर्ज करेगा जो खुदरा में FDI का विरोध करेगा और मीडिया में मेरे एजेंट उन सांसदों को भ्रष्ट बता देगा। 2 संसद अंदर जायेंगे तो 800 अपने आप पालन करेंगे मेरा।
..
==============
.
RM - तो आपका कहना लोकपाल भ्रष्टाचार खत्म नहीं करेगा ?
.
Lee Scott - नहीं भ्रष्टाचार काम होगा , पहले जो हम ज्यादा रिश्वत देनी पड़ती थी ,अब हमे कम देनी होगी कोई काम करवाने को। उदाहरण - पहले मुझे 800 सांसदों के लिए 3000 करोड़ देना होगा अब सिर्फ 100 करोड़ में काम हो जायेगा। तो भ्रष्टाचार कम हुआ के नहीं।
.
=============
.
RM -पर अन्ना कहते है जो लोकपाल रिश्वत लेगा उसे सुप्रीम कोर्ट के जज नौकरी से निकल देंगे ?
.
Lee Scott - में इतना बेवकूफ नहीं जो लोकपाल को कैमरा के सामने रिश्वत दूंगा। न ही लोकपाल जनता के बीच पैसे लेंगे। में या तो स्विस बैंक या मॉरिशस बैंक में पैसे जमा करवाऊंगा या उसके किसी रिश्तेदार को सलाहकार या वकील के तोर पे रखूँगा और उसको फीस के तोर पे रकम चुकाऊंगा।
.
हमने हजारो बार रिश्तोदारो को ऐसे ही रिश्वत खिलाई है भारत और बहोत से देशो में। हमारे पास सुप्रीम कोर्ट के जजो के बहोत से रिश्तेदार हमारे यहाँ वकील है। वे बहोत सक्षम है और धायण रखेंगे की हमारे एजेंट लोकपाल को कोई नुकसान न हो। ड्रामा खड़ा करने के लिए हम १-२ लोकपाल को फसा के निष्काषित करवा देंगे ताकि नागरिको की आस्था लोकपाल में बानी रहे।
.

.
RM - लेकिन लोगो को पता तो चल ही जायेगा ?
.
Lee Scott - पता चल भी जायेगा तो क्या। राइट टू रिकॉल तो है नहीं। जैसे भ्रस्ट मंत्री 5 साल नहीं हटते यह भी नहीं हटेंगे।
.
======================
.
RM - तो आप भारत में जनलोकपाल लाने के लिए क्या कदम उठा रहे है ?
.
Lee Scott - मेने और बहुराष्ट्रीय कम्पनियो के मालिको ने प्रचार अभियान के लिए 10000 करोड़ खर्च करने का फैसला लिया है। हम इक्विटी या लोन या विज्ञापन के नाम पर टीवी चैनेलो को पैसे देंगे जो अन्ना और लोकपाल को पॉजिटिव में दिखाए और इनका विरोध अकरने वालो को नेगेटिव में। हम, महात्मा अन्ना और लोकपाल को दिन के 48 घण्टे , सप्ताह के 14 दिन और साल के 100 सप्ताह दिखने के लिए पैसे देंगे। यहाँ तक की सास बहु सीरियल में भी अन्ना और लोकपाल नामसे टिकर चलेगा । टीवी चैनेलो का इस्तेमाल करके हम अन्ना को भगवन बना देंगे।
.
=================
.
RM - क्या आप राइट तो रिकॉल लोकपाल का समर्थन करते है ?
.
Lee Scott - ( गुस्से में और चिल्ला के ) कभी नहीं। फिर हमे 35 करोड़ नागरिको को रिश्वत देनी होगी। हम 100,200 या 5000 लोगो को रिश्वत खिला सकते है 35 करोड़ लोगो को नहीं। हम कभी नहीं चाहेंगे राइट तो रिकॉल लोकपाल का खंड लोकपाल ड्राफ्ट में आये।
.
====================
.
RM - क्या होगा अगर राइट तो रिकॉल लोकपाल का समर्थन करे।
.
Lee Scott - तो में मीडिया में उसकी कवरेज के लिए पैसे देना बांध कर दूंगा और मीडिया को बता दूंगा कभी राइट तो रिकॉल जैसा शब्द लोगो न पता चले। और मुझे नहीं लगता महात्मा अन्ना इसे स्वीकार करेंगे। उन्हें शोहरत चाहिए टीवी पर जो वो कभी खोना नहीं चाहेंगे।
.
========================
.
RM - क्या आप अन्ना और उनके लोगो को रिश्वत खिला रहे है ?
.

Lee Scott - नहीं। महात्मा अन्ना ने कभी पैसे नहीं मांगे और न ही मांगेंगे। वो 100 % ईमानदार है जब बात पैसे की हो। उनके सभी आदमी ईमानदार है। महात्मा अन्ना और उनके आदमियो को शोहरत चाहिए। महात्मा अन्ना को इतिहास की पुस्तको में अपना नाम चाहिए, नोबल प्राइज चाहिए। नेहरू और महत्मा गांधी जैसे बहोत से लोग है जो शोहरत के लिए देश हितो का बलिदान दे देंगे। हम टीवी चैनेलो को पैसा खिला के यकीन दिलवा देंगे महत्मा अन्ना भगवन है बदले में अन्ना हमे बिना राइट टू रिकॉल वाला जनलोकपाल लेन में मदद कर्नेगे !
.
==============
.
RM - तो अन्ना आपके एजेंट है ?
.
Lee Scott - एजेंट गलत शब्द होगा। महात्मा अन्ना हमारे एजेंट कभी नहीं बनेगे। वह हमारे सहजीवी साथी है - हम लोकपाल के अल्पाधिकार चाहते है वो प्रसिद्धि। हम उनकी मदद करेंगे वो हमारी मदद राइट टू रिकॉल के बिना लोकपाल लेन में करेंगे।
.
===================
.
RM -आपको लोकपाल ही चाहिए तो जंतर मंतर पर अनशन करने का विचार आपके मन में क्यों नहीं आया ?
.
Lee Scott - में खुश हूँ आपने यह प्रशन पूछा। असल में मैं तयारी कर रहा था। टीवी चैनेलो को 2000 करोड़ रुप्पय्ये के भुगतान का विचार बना रहा था ताकि वे मेरे लिए भावनात्मक लहर पैदा करे। तुम्हे पता है में $ 50,000 का अरमानी सूट , $ 10,000 की टाई , $ 5000 का शर्ट , $ 20.000 के गुसी के जूते , पहनता हूँ। पर अनशन के लिए मेने $2000 का अरमानी सूट , सिर्फ $10 की टाई , $50 का शर्ट , और $500के जूते ख़रीदे। पर मेरे सलाहकारों ने कहा आप 1000 बार अनशन अकरके मर जायेंगे पर को भारतीय अप्पकी आवाज नहीं सुनेगा। इसके लिए आपको ऐसे व्यक्ति की जरुरत है जो भारतीयों को रुला सके। व्यक्ति साधारण कपडे पहनता हो , आमिर नहीं होना चाहिए , बुजुर्ग हो , और उसका अतीत अछा हो। तो मेने अपना फैसला बदला और मीडिया को 2000 करोड़ रूपपए दिए महत्मा अन्ना को बताने के लिए। जिन रामदेव पर रत 2 बजे लाठिया बरसी मेने अन्ना को सुबह 15 अगस्त को 8 बजे गिरफ्तार करवाया . जब पूरा देश टीवी देख रहा हो।
.=============
RM - appka धन्यवाद ली स्कॉट।
.
ली स्कॉट - welcome , और धन्यवाद भारतीय नागरिको को यह बताने के लिए बिना राइट टू रिकॉल के जनलोकपाल कितना खतरनाक साबित होगा।
.नोट - : यह इंटरव्यू काल्पनिक है। यह इस सन्दर्भ में दिया गया है की लोकपाल का ड्रामा कार्यकर्ता या नागरिक आसानी से समझ सके।
===========================

Wed Aug 03 2016 14:51:35 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Please buy tomorrow, Indian Express, page-2 , Ahmedabad, Delhi, Mumbai, Pune, Kokata and Financial Express Banglaore page-3.
.
We have following advertisement requesting voters to send necessary orders to MPs via sms/twitter, and requesting MPs how they can setup sms receiving servers. And how the communication over sms / twitter is NOT naarebaazi and it is systematic.
.
The ad is summary of the TECHNICAL description of the system. And so the advt is technical in nature. The ad is given to tell all activists that we are serious about the concept. As per getting complete understanding, one will need to see the links given in the advt
.
Here is the text of the advt
.
advertisement from Right to Recall Party (unregistered)
Right to Recall Party (to be registered)
.
(2) Request to 85 crore voters of India – (2A) pls start sending necessary orders, to PM \ CM \ MP \ MLA \ Mayor via sms\twitter, about printing necessary laws (2B) sms must have internet link, filehash (2C) pls ask PM\CMs\MPs to make 2 sms receiving servers – 1st server will keep orders sent by voters confidential and show only count ; 2nd server will put sms sent by voters with their voterids, on publicly visible Govt website
.
(1) Request to PM, CMs, MPs etc – (1A) pls put your official cellno and twitterid on govt websites, so that 85 crore voters can send you orders via sms\twitter (1B) pls make 2 servers – 1st will show filehash and count of sms received ; 2nd will show voterids as well
.
(4) To all - movie Rustom is wrong. Jury System was ended not due to Nanavati case, but only because Jurors across India were siding with landless and giving verdicts against landlords
.
-------------------------
.

From : Rahul Chimanbhai Mehta, B Tech, Computer Science, IIT Delhi; MS, Computer Science, Rutgers Univ, USA; facebook.com\MehtaRahulC
.
____________________
.
For full details on ‘order your MP via sms’, see tinyurl.com\OrderMpSms For videos on several law-drafts proposed by we at Right to Recall Party, see youtube.com/RightToRecallGroup , facebook.com\MehtaRahulC
.
_____________________________________
.

(1) Request to PM, CMs, MPs, MLAs, Mayors etc –
.
(1A) we activists at Right to Recall Party are now openly requesting all 85 crore voters of India to do their constitutional \ religious \ moral duty, to send necessary orders to MPs, MLAs etc via sms\twitter
.
(1B) orders via sms may be about laws that voters need you to print
.
(1C) so we request all to publish official cellno and twitterid of all MPs\MLAs etc on govt \ party websites, so sms don’t come on personal cellno, and you don’t get disturbed
.
(1D) we will request voters to include url = internet link and BTH = bittorrenthash of law-drafts voters want
.
(1E) we also request you to setup sms counting servers to count number of sms containing specific link \ BTH
.
(1F) so if lakhs of voters send you crores of sms, ordering you to print say 1000 laws they need, then you WILL NOT have to read all crores of sms
.
(1G) but server will give you links/BTH that appear in sms of over 8 lakh voters in your Loksabha constituency, and you may read only law-drafts connected to those links / BTH
.
(1H) so ‘order your MP via sms’ is not noise proposal like slogan shouting, dharana; if you MPs etc implement sms counting server, then it is structured procedure to get verifiable opinion count .
.
(1i) PM, CM can legally put the voterids / names of all voters (who have sent a specific url / BTH) on a Govt website, so that all voters can verify that list is correct
.
(1J) see smstoneta.com for demo
.
(1K) tinyurl.com\OrderMpSms has more details
.
____________________
.
(2) Request to all 85 crore voters of India –
.
(2A) its your constitutional, religious, moral duty to send necessary orders to PM, CMs, MPs, MLAs, Mayors, Sarpanchs etc via sms\twitter
.
(2B) you must not send sms\tweet to MPs’ personal cellno \ twitterid; you should sms \ tweet only to his official cellno \ twitterid
.
(2C) if MP hasn’t published his official cellno, then pls send sms \ tweet to cellno\twitterid of party president or nearby MP of his party
.
(2D) IMO sms you send must only be about law-drafts you want and not about problems, slogans
.
(2E) sms must have internet link to law-draft and BTH = bitorrenthash of text/pdf file having law-draft you want
.
(2F)1st sms you send should be voterid
.
(2G) pls also ask party heads over twitter to put official cellno\twitterid of their MPs, MLAs etc on party website
.
(2G) pls ask PM/CM/MPs to make server which shows sms sent from voters, along with voterids so that voters can verify ; for this, send them sms ‘ tinyurl.com/TcpIndia bth=e65f846014a5c823dc521392bbf57b97fcf20dcb‘

-----------------------------------------

(3) example :
.
(3A) USA is ahead of India, because USA has Jury System, Right to Recall CMs\judges\PoliceChiefs, multi-elections, referendum, has wealth tax, has no GST
.
(3B) so, if voters wish to bring JurySys in India, then they can send this order to MP via sms\twitter “ tinyurl.com/JurySys2 , filehash = b3035ee77f7eba8c32785a2d97819a73f2f0a705 , bittorrenthash = 020fc292568ba0e079db12635218a9a23180b611 “. As more voters send this sms, chances that MPs will have to print Jury System law increases
.
---------------------------------------
.
(4) Rustom is wrong -
.
(4A) JurySys was ended by Nehru in 1959, not due to Nanavati case, but to protect large landlords
.
(4B) Back then, Jurors across India were siding with landless and were giving verdicts against landlords
.
(4C) so ending JurySys had become necessary to protect large landlords
.
(4D) also, Jurors acquitted Kavas Nanavati not due to media influence, but because Jurors saw Ahuja as “paternity fraudster criminal” and saw Nanavati as victim of “potential paternity frauding criminal”
.
(4E) see details on Nanavati case and details on “JurySys was ended to protect landlords not due to Nanavati case” at tinyurl.com/JurySys4
.
___________________________
.
(5) answers to technical FAQs :
.
(5A) phno can be tagged with voterid; so if a voter has 2 phno, then system will count only one phno
.
(5B) sms and tweeting is better than signature campaign as (5b1) signatures can be forged (5b2) signature counting cant be automated (5b3) if leaders sell out, then signature campaign dies; but leader sell out cant kill sms campaign (5b4) so “order MP via sms with filehash” is better than signature campaign
.
-------------------
.
(6) answers to political FAQs : I have no links with AAP, AK = Kejrival. I started campaign for Right to Recall in oct-1998. Four AAP MPs havent put any RTR bill in Loksabha till date. So AK has no interest in RTR MP, RTR District Police Chief laws etc. All in all, I have no links with AK, AAP
.

A question for recallists ,
.
Many moons ago, in late 1970s, I came across a well off person in 50s. who said that "so called freedom movement contributed nothing to me. I was born in absolutely poor family. I studied hard in a run a down school. I gave bank clerk exam, and got selected. Later I took external courses, studied hard and gave officer exam, and got selected as officer. And I have come on my own".
.
Was he missing something? Or are we missing something when we praise freedom fighters?
.
My response , which I developed some 20 years after we had conversation was --- the procedure of recruitment by written exam came because of activists.
.
Has there been no activism and resulting resistance, the elitemen would have continued with recruitment procedure by interviews where they can recruit their relatives etc. Or they may even make it hereditary.
.
So he was intelligent, studious and hard working. Which is why he excelled in written exam. But if procedure of recruitment by written exam was not there, he may have never even become a clerk. In fact, his preparation for exams made him smarter. If there was no procedure of recruitment by written exams, he perhaps would NOT even had prepared for exams, and never improved his mathematical abilities etc.
.
Now activist may not have demanded Recruitment by written exam. But activists only gather resistance against nepotism, And then some elitemen suggests that recruitment should be by written exams. And other elitemen agree with him due to resistance,. i.e. in absence of resistance, the other elitemen would have never accepted recruitment by written exams. So out of 10 small states, each facing resistance from commons, one state may finally accept recruitment by written exam. Then that state will become stronger and defeat all other states !!! And so this procedure will spread.
.
So freedom fighters created a situation where the British had to give higher share of powers to freedom fighters, and not all of them were like Jawaher Ghazi,. Some due to fear and some due to commitment extended recruitment by written exam to many many departments, and so chances that studious persons will get selected increased
..
-----------------------------
.

Thu Aug 04 2016 16:10:36 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

One more question for recallists -- pls google on sortition. It means filling a position in govt using random selection. eg we have proposed sortition in Jury.
.
So election of 543 MPs be replaced by sortition i.e. randomly select 543 MPs and make them MP, and if one goes defcunt / corrupt, then Jury of say 1500 voters can expel him, but replacement will come again via random selection. IOW, no election of MPs or no mass opinon gathering?
.
Please take this as no joke. Because Athence used sortition and many across world and in India are working on this proposal.
.
We have proposed election + recall to fill 543 MP positions and many more such positions such MLA, Panchayats, PM, CM Rajsabha MPs etc. We have NO proposed random selection of MPs.
.
Election has known demerit --- schemers and money power dominate. A schemer and a man with sponsor is more likely to win election than someone who isnt schemer. So if MPs are chosen via election, then many of them will have nexuses with elitemen. While random selection removes possibility of all random selection.
.
So if we say --- select 1500 Jurors from voter list as random, then why not chose 543 MPs from voter list at random? Or why not chose district panchayat members using random selection?
.
So what do YOU support --- election vs sortition?
.
And what political camapign method would YOU take, if an activist proposed sortition and claims that sortion will have less nexused MPs than (election + recall)
.
---------------
.
Added after 1 day
.
My argument against sortition - we can .combine sortion and election
.
i.e. lets have 543*3 MPs, --- one third are elected with preferential voting system, 1/3rd are recruited by written exams, and one third are selected at random.
.
Sortition - Lets say India has 85 crore voters, and India is divided into say 453 Constituency, and each constituency has say 15.6 lakh voters . Say 543 MPs are chosen at random from whole India, or one person is chosen at random from each constituency or N persons are chosen from each state, where N = number of constituency in each state. After 5 years, there will be re-sortition. And if MP is bad, thena Jury of 500 will decide if he should continue or expelled. And if expelled, then new person is chosen at random
.
Election -- Each constitution elects one MP
.
-------------------
.
Now in election, merit will be better. In India, merit in election gets lowered because if a person wants to stop SoGa or elect NaMo as PM, he has no option but to vote for BJP candidate as MP. And if he wants to stop NaMo or he wants SoGa to become PM, then he has no option but to vote for Congress. And same for most parties.
.
So parties know this limitation, that voter has limited choice. So they dont hesitate in putting less intelligent person as candidate.
.
But consider USA, where Parliament elections are separate from President. So if one has wants say Trump as President, he can vote for Trump, and later he can vote for a Democrat as Senator. So both parties have to put better candidates, may not be best. But level is better than the case when MP/PM elections are clubbed.
.
So today, quality of MP in India is poor because and one reason is that MP / PM election are clubbed, Not because of procedure of election per se,
.
Another is --- a voter wants to stop A from getting elected , and so he doesnt vote for X whom he thinks is best but votes for B who can possibly stop A. This problem reduces with preferntial voting system aka instant runoff voting
.
So lets consider systems where
.
(i), MPs are elected separately from PM, elected with preferential voting system and are recallable. And TCP is there to print the laws that people want and MPs are refusing to print.
.
(ii) random selection
.
Here election will give better results than sortion.
.
Why?
.
Sortition will give people with average intelligence, and some half will be bewlo average. Where as system where each candidate is elected on his own, the IQ level will be higher. He may be schemer, he may be nexused, but he will be smarter. And sometimes, to make laws and deal with foreign enemies. one may need smarter person even if schemer
.
Also, lets say a person has proved to be a good law-drafter. Then sortion allows no way to retain him. Whereas election allows people to retain him.
.
And lets say if a MP has nexuses. Then when he writes law-drafts, the nexuses will become visible. So at that time, voters can expel him.
.
The problem of unfair mediamen or rather paidmediamen is because of lack of RTR over Doordarshan-Chairman, and it is NOT due to election system. The absence of RTR over Doordarshan-Chairman has worsened election system, and ensured that chances of nexused candidate winning increases. And so due to absence of RTR over Doordarshan Chairman, sortion has edge over election. But once RTR comes over Doordarshan Chairman, the edge sortion has over election, along the dimension of nexuslessness will reduce
.
And in my proposal sets, there is sortion for final passing of the law. How? In chap-40, I have proposed the law taht law passed by MPs will be sent to each tahsil where 15 persons will be chosen at random. And if majority Jurors oppose this law, then law will be considered as cancelled by that Tahsil. And if majority tahsil cancel that law passed by MPs, then that law will be cancelled
.
====
.
But finally, sortion has one point which best election system cant possibly have --- highest degree of nexulessness and least amoun or zero amount of scheming.
.
------
.
Also, during election, candidate makes promise and so he can be asked to fulfill the promises. And election + recall ensures that he wont make wild promises and he works to fulfill the promises. But an MP selected by sortition never made promises, and so he cant be asked to work to keep the.
.
And the process of election will force candidates to come up with solutions. So many solution proposals which come due to elections wont come due to sortition.
.
So solution I propose is
.
(1) Let each constituency have 3 MPs
.
(2) One is elected using preferential voting system, one is selected by written exam and third is selected using random selection.
.
(3) Each of of the three is recallable by majority and / or Jury of 500 voters. But if recalled, then next one will be selected by same method
.
(4) Law passed MPs will be sent to Tahsils Juries and if Tahsil Juries cancel it, them it will be cancelled.
.
So there is no harm in accommodating sortition. And election system can also stay. On some issues, the MPs selected by randomness , due to their nexuslessness, may outperform elected MPs.
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Aug 05 2016 06:48:56 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

GST ------ MTP = Missing Trader Problem, proposed solutions of WT and CAM, and how GST without CAM will create slaughter of small / middle businesses
.
List of chapters
(1) Missing trader problem
(2) How MTP will slaughter small businesses / manufacturers
(3) "Repeal GST and bring Wealth tax" as solution
(4) CAM = "Controlled Account Method" within GST framework
(5) Giving GST paid as deduction in Income Tax payabl
(6) What can YOU do to bring WT or CAM?
.
=============
.
chap-1 : Missing trader problem
---------------------------
.
GST has following problem

(1.1) Say a trader/manufacturer B buys goods from A and say they agree for base price of Rs 1000. Say tax rate is say 20%. Then A's invoice will be Rs 1000 base price + Rs 200 GST = Rs 1200 total
.
(1.2) If B is end user or "lumpsump dealer" then there is no issue. But say B is a trader or manufacturer who pays GST at 20% rate.
.
(1.3). Say C buys goods from B and say they agree for base price of Rs 1050. Say tax rate is say 20%. Then A's invoice will be Rs 1050 base price + Rs 210 GST = Rs 1260 total
.
(1.4) B has to make a statement as
GST collected from C = Rs 210
GST paid via A = INPUT CREDIT = Rs 200
GST to be paid = Rs 10
And B has to pay Rs 10
.
(1.5) But if A doesnt pay Rs 200, then B will have to pay Rs 200 again, for all practical purposes. i.e. input credit of Rs 200 will denied.
.
Basically, A is the missing trader. And the event that he didnt pay GST is what I called as "Missing Trader Problem".
.
The two solution I propose are wealth tax and Tax-accounts
.
======
.
chap-2 : How MTP will slaughter small /medium businesses?
-------------------------------------------------------
.
Consider a town where there are 1000 small / middle traders and manufacturers , named as as S1 to S1000 and say 10 big traders and manufacturers.say L1 to L10.
.
Say S1 to S10 collect GST from their 10000 buyers across India named as B1 to B10000. Say S1 to S10 didnt pay GST. So these 10000 buyers will have to pay GST again. so other sellers S11 to S1000 were honest, but S1 to S10 were dishonest.
.
Now say same buyers also bought goods/services etc from L1 to L10. Now each of L1 to L10 will NOT default on GST,. The Large companies will never do such petty white collar fraud. If they want to do fraud, they will bribe Minister and get land for cheap, they will bribe bank CEO and get loans and do NPA, and so on. But they will not do a trackable criminal fraud. Thats because large companies have a lot of asset and they can disappear in month.
So in short, some 1% small / middle business will do Missing Trader Fraud i.e. will not pay GST they collected. And none of the large seller will create MTP.
.
Now every buyer will see than 1 to 3 out of 100 small traders / manufacturers create MTP, while none of the large trader / manufacturer do MTP.
.
So those who have been cheated by 1-2 small trader / manufacturers will lose faith in ALL small traders / manufacturers !! and they will start dealing with large trader / manufacturer as far as possible.
.
So GST with solution to MTP will slaughter small / medium, business and manufacturers.
.
So for first 3 years, GST will look great --- tax returns will become simple and businesses will face lesser hassles. But after 2-3 years, missing trader notices will start coming, and the slaughter of smal / medium business will start.
.
====
.
chap-3 : Wealth tax as solution
------------------------------
.
I have proposed removal of excise, cst, sales tax, vat, service tax, gst and replace them by WTMC = Wealth tax minus credits
.
I have proposed wealth tax on urban properties as follows
.
1% of land's circle rate
plus 1% of construction's actual or circile rate (higher)
minus 250 sqft per person (500 sqft for age above 60 years)
minus 500 sqft per person (1000 sqft for age above 60 years)
minus 15% of salaries paid
minus income tax paid on non-salary incomes
minus 50% of income tax paid on salary income
minus ALL taxes paid such VAT, excise etc as long as they are
minus taxes paid on fuel , electricity if any
minus wealth tax credit of previous year
.
If above is negative, then it will get added to WTC = "wealth tax credit" for next year. So WTC = is cumulative

And second proposal is to remove several exemptions from income tax such as SEZ, Mauritius route, tax exemption to infrastructure projects etc
.
So GST gets repealed and above WTMC plus streamlined income tax will give more revenue than GST. Also it is less regressive, and will bring down land prices.

I would soon publish link , filehash and bittorrenthash for wealth tax, that we voters can send to PM \ MPs via sms\twitter. The goal should be to convince over 45 crore voters to send sms to PM\MPs, and to convince atleast 10 crore voters to send tweets to MPs\PM.
.
Please click "follow" on my FB profile, I will soon publish the full SMS, and files with full draft of this wealth tax proposal.
.
====
.
chap-4 : solution within GST framework - CAM
---------------------------------------------
.
The second solution I propose is CAM.= Controlled Account Method. Here, the bank will provide an account named as CAM-account. It will have following features
,.
(4.1) Say CAM is owned by trader or manufacturer X
.
(4.2) Anyone , X or anyone, can deposit money to CAM.
.
(4.3) X can move funds from CAM only to another CAM account of GST Govt Account, and not where else
.
(4.4) The GST Govt Officer can move funds from CAM account to private account of X or heir of X or GST's Govt account , and no where else
.
(4.5) The interest on CAM account will be same as savings account, and it will be same as one year FD on minimum balance of 1 year, and no more.
.
(4.6) Now CAM solves MTP. How? Say B has to pay Rs 1000 base price + Rs 200 GST to A. Then B can write two cheque to A --
.
(4.6.1) pay Rs 1000 to current or savings account of A
.
(4.6.2) pay Rs 200 to CAM-account of A
.
(4.6.3) Bank can support multi-receiver cheque , where cequere writer can write both account numbers on same piece of paper, and also write details such as invoice number etc
.
So now trader A CANNOT siphon money from CAM account to his own account. Because money in CAM account can be used only to deposit into another CAM account or pay GST account of Govt. Or money will stay in A's CAM account, which govt can later recover from bank. So in any case, B will not suffer due to misaction or inaction of A.
.
IOW, CAM-account is owned by A, and all deposits are made by A or by others behalf of GST due on A, But A cant make withdrawals except to pay GST to his sellers or to Govt. And Govt can take money out depending on situation. But that will be Govt's action, and wont create liability on buyers.
.
Traders and manufacturers have been suffering from MTP in VAT , CAT etc since 2013 in all states across India. Sadly, no State Govt asked banks to implement CAM
.
I would soon publish link , filehash and bittorrenthash for CAM Account, that we voters can send to PM\MPs via sms\twitter. The goal should be to convince over 45 crore voters to send sms to PM\MPs, and to convince atleast 10 crore voters to send tweets to MPs\PM.
.
Please click "follow" on my FB profile, I will soon publish the full SMS, and files with full draft of this wealth tax proposal.
.
===========
.
(5) Giving GST paid as deduction in Income Tax payable
-----------------------------------
.
A separate proposal of ours is to let all --- end users as well as traders / manufacturers, to deduct GST from income tax payable. This can be implemented even for end users. When an an end user pays say Rs 1000 price + Rs 200 GST, he can pay by cheque which has two lines --- price and GST. And bank will track the GST he has paid. And likewise, when he pays by card, he the card will also track GST he has paid. and when user pays by cash, he can give his card, and the card will not make any actual payment, but will record "price and GST paid in cash". And hen seller uploads his cashbook with tax dept, the GST dept can cross check. And if buyer doesnt give card at the time of buying or even at a later date, then that GST paid will become Govt property and cannot be reclaimed,
.
So GST paid by each citizen and each business, can be tracked, added , and later can be given as deduction from income tax payable.
.
But please not --- this proposal reduces double taxation ; this proposal has NOTHING to with MTP of GST or GST per se,
.
I would soon publish link , filehash and bittorrenthash for this "GST paid can be deducted from income tax payable" proposal. We voters can send these link etc to PM\MPs via sms\twitter. The goal should be to convince over 45 crore voters to send sms to PM\MPs, and to convince atleast 10 crore voters to send tweets to MPs\PM.
.
Please click "follow" on my FB profile, I will soon publish the full SMS, and files with full draft of this wealth tax proposal.
.

===============
.

----------------------------------
.
IMO, you can ask MPs and all party workers to provide DRAFT, for the solution they propose to Missing Trader Problem in GST.
.
And if you like the wealth tax solution or CAM solution, we have proposed then pls click follow on my profile. And pls like / share this status. I will soon publish full draft, link, filehash and bittorrenthash that you can send to PM\MP.

say, 1% journalist, 1% leaders, 1% political science professors, 1% economics professor, 1% economists and 1% activists are 100% UNPAID and 100% honest and rest 99% in all category are paid.

Question ---- So what are the damages in following statements

(a) every judge is corrupt / nexused / nepotic judge

Sun Aug 07 2016 13:52:36 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

Why make this DCJurySysMAAGC draft ?
.
This draft is a subset of draft DCJurySys. The DCJurySys puts Jury Trial in all cases, while this DCJurySysMAAGC law-draft puts Jury Trial only for some specific crimes such murder, crimes against women, adulteration etc. So if DCJurySys gets passed, then this DCJurySysMAAGC law-draft is not needed. But if DcJurySys has not passed, then this law-draft creates a change -- that murder etc cases will be tried by Juries and Juries only. But other cases will remain judges till law-draft is printed to put Jury in those cases as well.
.
Now a question comes - if DCJurySys covers all cases, then why is this draft needed at all?
.
Answer is - in activism, DCJurySysMAAGC helps !!! IOW, DCJurySysMAAGC has huge merit from activism PoV.
.
Many anti-Jury folks say that 'Jury System will result into a huge damage because commons dont know law and so commons will give all wrong verdicts.', And many persons who are less familiar with JurySys get confused and scared with this argument. And hesitate in supporting Jury System.

So here 'JurySys over murder, abduction cases etc' comes to help.
.
So now say the activist is campaigning for "JurySys over murder etc cases" to an audience from general population. And say if an anti-Jury person makes argument that "commons dont know law". Then the pro-Jury activist can put a counter argument before audience that "see, I am advocating Jury system over cases of murder, abduction, adulteration etc ; and so statements that commons do know what is murder etc and what are laws about murder etc dont apply ; and even then , this anti-Jury person is saying that commons dont know about murder, abduction, adulteration etc and so IMO, this anti-Jury is person is wrong".
.
Now many in audience will see that commons do know what is murder, what is abduction, what is adulteration etc etc. And so the anti-Jury person, who says that "commons dont know laws on murder etc" will look bad. And anti-Jury person will NOT be able convince the audience that "There should be no Jury Trial over murder cases".
.
So what problem does DCJurySysMAAGC reduce?
.
It makes it easy for pro-Jury activists to convince audience to bring Jury System,over one type of case at a time. And with that, Jury System can come over ALL types of cases.
.
So DCJurySysMAAGC has plus point from activism point of view.
.
And basically, I have kept one law-draft called as DCJurySys which is JurySys for all cases
.
And then there are 100 law-drafts called' as
.
(1) JurySys over murder, abduction, adulteration, gohatya, corruption
.
(2) JurySys over corruption, atrocity, inefficiency in policemen
.
(3) JurySys over corruption / atrocity / inefficiency in govt hospitals
.
(4) JurySys over corruption / inefficiency in govt schools
.
(5) JurySys over corruption / inefficiency in hospitals
.
(6) JurySys over employee employer and labor cases
.
(7) JurySys over GST evasion and corruption of GST staff
.
(8) JurySys over family dispute and deciding alimony and child custody
.
and so on.
.
IOW, when activist is campaigning for all these JurySys, the anti-Jury person will no longer be able to say "Jury doesnt know what is atrocity", "Jury doesnt know what is corruption" and "Jury doesnt what is family" so on !!!
.
So in some cases, pro-Jury activists may be able to convince the voters to send orders to MPs via sms with hashcodes of those Jury Systems. So chances that Jury System will come increases
.

Rahul Mehta Gandhinagar LsCandidate

Fri Aug 12 2016 01:54:31 GMT+0000 (Coordinated Universal Time)

सुहेल देव पाठक:

■ सुब्रमण्यम स्वामी पिछले 17 साल से कर रहे है मुकदमे,आजतक एक आरोपी को नही मिली सज़ा
.
◆ सुब्रमण्यम स्वामी पिछले कुछ वर्षो से काफी चर्चा का विषय रहे है, उन्होंने अपने कई भाषणों में दावे कर चुके है कि वह अदालतों में मुकदमे करके भ्रष्टाचारीयो के खिलाफ लड़ रहे है और उनके पास ऐसे-ऐसे सबूत है जिससे वह 2-3 महीनो में सभी भ्रष्ट नेताओ,अधिकारियों को एक-एक करके जेल भेजेंगे
.
◆परन्तू स्वामी के सभी दावो की हवा निकल चुकी है क्योकि वह 1998 से मुकदमे कर रहे है जिसमे आजतक एक भी बड़े नेता,अधिकारी को सज़ा नही मिली चाहे वो जयललिता विवाद, 2G घोटाला,फ़र्ज़ी डिग्री/पासपोर्ट हो या अन्य।
.
◆ ऐसे में जनता का स्वामी पर से विश्वास उठने लगा है जोकि कभी स्वामी की दीवानी थी और उन्हें क्रन्तिकारी और युगपुरुष समझती थी। उनके ऊपर सवाल भी उठाये जा रहे कि उनके काम में कोई पारदर्शिता नही है, स्वामी ने आजतक एक भी सबूत सार्वजनिक नही किया है और वह जनता को केवल भाषण सुनाने और मीडिया में बड़ी-बड़ी बाते करने के अलावा कुछ नही करते।
.
◆विशेषज्ञो का मानना है कि- कोई नेता,अधिकारी इतना बड़ा मुर्ख नही होता जो भ्रष्टाचार के सबूत सरकारी कागजो में छोड़कर जायेगा,स्वामी केवल लोगो का टाइम-पास करने और खुद को लोकप्रिय बनाने के लिये मुकदमा करते है ताकि भविष्य में उन्हें कोई मंत्री-पद मिल सके, यदि सबूत है तो सार्वजनिक क्यों नही कर देते ???
.
● यदि भ्रष्टाचार के पुख्ता सबूत है तो फेसबुक,ट्विटर,वेबसाइट पर सबूत को सार्वजनिक क्यों नही करते???
.
●आरोपियों का पब्लिक में नार्को टेस्ट लेने की मांग का हमेशा से विरोध करते है स्वामी।
.
● विरोधियों ने आरोप लगाया कि सस्ती लोकप्रियता पाने के लिये ड्रामा करते है और पर्दे के पीछे आरोपियों से कर लेते है सेटिंग।
.
● जजो से अपने मुकदमो की वीडियो रिकॉर्डिंग की मांग कभी नही करते सुब्रमण्यम स्वामी
.
===============
■समाधान: भारतीय नागरिको को नेताओ,मंत्रियो के ऊपर निर्भर नही रहना चाहिये (नेता अपने फायदे के लिये दुश्मनो से भी हाथ मिला लेते है) और खुद अच्छे कानूनों को सांसद पर दबाव बनाकर राजकीय राजपत्र में छपवाने का प्रयत्न करना चाहिये जिससे भ्रष्टाचार,अपराध,गरीबी,बेरोजगारी,विदेशी कंपनियों की तानाशाही खत्म हो सके
.
★राईट टू रिकॉल समूह द्वारा प्रस्तावित कानूनी ड्राफ्ट इस लिंक में देखे : https://web.facebook.com/notes/459989734191922/ इन कानूनी ड्राफ्टों का अध्ययन करे,अगर देशहित में अच्छे लगे तो अपने नेताओ को ये लिंक SMS द्वारा भेजकर आदेश दे की इन कानूनों को मुख्यमंत्री/प्रधानमंत्री पर दबाव बनाये कि इसे राजकीय राजपत्र में छाप कर लागू किया जाये
.

Proposed law drafts - Hindi:

[ टीसीपी - इंडिया ]
.
भारत के सभी नागरिकों,
.
यह पोस्ट टीसीपी क़ानून के प्रस्तावित कानूनी ड्राफ्ट का विवरण प्रस्तुत करता है।
.
यदि आप इस कानूनी ड्राफ्ट का समर्थन करते है तो अपने सांसद को एसएमएस द्वारा इस पोस्ट का लिंक भेजें। तथा इस कानूनी ड्राफ्ट की जानकारी देश के नागरिको तक पहुंचाने के लिए इसे लाइक करें, शेयर करें, समाचार पत्रो में विज्ञापन दें, इस मुद्दे पर चुनाव लड़ें तथा इस पोस्ट के पहले कमेंट में सुझायी गयी अन्य गतिविधियों में भाग लें। यदि आप इस पोस्ट के पहले तथा दूसरे कमेंट को नहीं देख पा रहे है तो, इस पेज के विवरण खंड (डिस्क्रिप्शन कॉलम) को देखें। वहाँ इस पेज के सभी पोस्ट्स तथा शेष पांच कमेंट्स के लिंक की सूची दी गयी है।
.
यदि करोड़ो नागरिक अपने सांसद को अपने मोबाईल फोन से एसएमएस भेजते है तो देश की वर्तमान व्यवस्था में क्या परिवर्तन आयेंगे ? इस प्रश्न तथा ऐसे ही अक्सर पूछे जाने वाले अन्य प्रश्नो के जवाब इस पोस्ट के दूसरे कमेंट में देखें जा सकते है। अन्य सम्बंधित जानकारी के लिए इस समुदाय का विवरण खंड देखें।
.
टिप्पणी -- इस क़ानून ड्राफ्ट की विश्वसनीयता बनाएं रखने के लिए, इस पोस्ट को एक बार लिखे जाने के बाद संपादित नही किया गया है। वर्तनी या व्याकरण आदि की अशुद्धियों के सम्बन्ध में कृपया इस पोस्ट का छठा कमेंट देखें।
.
==========
.
सांसद को भेजे जाने वाले SMS का प्रारूप --
.
"Hon MP, I order you to -- https://web.facebook.com/ProposedLawsHindi/posts/529823220529210, voter ID : ####"
.
==========
.
माननीय सांसद,
.
यदि आपको इस पोस्ट का लिंक एसएमएस द्वारा प्राप्त होता है तो, ऐसा एसएमएस आपके लिए मतदाता द्वारा भेजा गया आदेश है। इस पोस्ट के लेखक का भेजे गए ऐसे आदेश या एसएमएस से कोई लेना देना नहीं है। आपको भेजा गया ऐसा आदेश इस कानूनी ड्राफ्ट को भारत के राजपत्र में प्रकाशित करने के लिए दिया गया है।
.

.

========राजपत्र अधिसूचना का प्रारम्भ ========
.
पारदर्शी शिकायत प्रणाली - टीसीपी के लिए प्रस्तावित अधिसूचना
.
1. [ जिला कलेक्टर या उसके क्लर्क के लिए निर्देश ]

राष्ट्रपति कलेक्टर को आदेश देते है कि : कोई भी महिला मतदाता, दलित मतदाता, वरिष्ठ नागरिक मतदाता, गरीब मतदाता, किसान मतदाता या कोई भी नागरिक मतदाता यदि जिला कलेक्टर कार्यालय में उपस्थित कोई शिकायत या प्रस्ताव शपथपत्र के माध्यम से प्रस्तुत करता है, तो कलेक्टर उस शपथपत्र को 20 रूपये प्रति पृष्ठ की दर से शुल्क लेकर दर्ज करेगा और सीरियल नंबर के साथ एक रसीद जारी करेगा। तथा कलेक्टर इस शपथपत्र को स्कैन करके शपथपत्र प्रस्तुतकर्ता की मतदाता संख्या के साथ प्रधानमन्त्री की वेबसाइट पर रखेगा, ताकि कोई भी नागरिक इस अर्जी को बिना लॉग इन के देख सके।
.
2. [ पटवारी या तलाटी के लिए निर्देश ] राष्ट्रपति पटवारी को आदेश देते है कि :
.
(2.1) कोई महिला मतदाता, दलित मतदाता, वरिष्ठ नागरिक मतदाता, गरीब मतदाता, किसान मतदाता या कोई भी नागरिक मतदाता यदि धारा-1 के तहत प्रस्तुत किये गए किसी शपथपत्र पर आपनी 'हाँ' या 'ना' दर्ज कराने के लिए मतदाता पहचान पत्र के साथ पटवारी कार्यालय में आता है, तो पटवारी 3 रुपये का शुल्क लेकर कंप्यूटर में मतदाता की हाँ/ना को उसकी मतदाता पहचान संख्या के साथ दर्ज करेगा, तथा मतदाता को इसकी एक रसीद देगा। पटवारी नागरिक की हाँ/ना को उसकी मतदाता संख्या के साथ प्रधानमन्त्री की वेबसाइट पर भी रखेगा।
.
(2.2) नागरिक पटवारी के दफ्तर जाकर किसी भी दिन अपनी 'हाँ' या 'ना' तीन रुपये का शुल्क देकर बदल सकेगा।
.
(2.3) बीपीएल कार्ड धारक के लिए देय शुल्क 1 रू होगा।
.
(2.4) कलेक्टर ऐसा सिस्टम बना सकेगा जिससे मतदाता द्वारा दर्ज की गयी हाँ/ना की पुष्टि का एस.एम.एस उसके रजिस्टर्ड मोबाइल पर भेजा जा सके।
.
(2.5) कलेक्टर एक ऐसा सिस्टम बना सकता है, जिससे मतदाता का फोटो तथा अंगुलीयों की छाप को रसीद पर डाला जा सके।
.
(2.6) प्रधानमंत्री ऐसा सिस्टम बना सकेंगे जिससे मतदाता अपनी हाँ/ना 50 पैसे का शुल्क देकर एटीएम द्वारा दर्ज करवा सके।
.
(2.7) प्रधानमंत्री एक ऐसा सिस्टम बना सकते है, जिससे मतदाता अपनी हाँ/ना 5 पैसे देकर एस.एम.एस. द्वारा दर्ज कर सके।
.
3. [ सभी नागरिको, अधिकारियों, मंत्रियों, न्यायधीशों के लिए निर्देश ]
.
(3.1) ये प्रक्रिया कोई जनमत-संग्रह या रेफेरेंडम नहीं है। मतदाताओ द्वारा दर्ज की गयी हाँ/ना किसी भी अधिकारी, मंत्री, जज, सांसद, विधायक आदि पर बाध्यकारी नहीं है। यदि भारत के 37 करोड़ नागरिक मतदाता किसी शपथपत्र पर हाँ दर्ज कर देते है तो प्रधानमंत्री उस शपथपत्र पर कार्यवाही कर सकते है या ऐसा करना उनके लिए जरूरी नहीं है, या प्रधानमंत्री इस्तीफा दे सकते है, या उन्हें ऐसा करने की जरुरत नहीं है। प्रधानमन्त्री का निर्णय ही अंतिम होगा।
.
(3.2) कलेक्टर धारा 2 के तहत दर्ज किये जाने वाले हाँ/ना को पंजीकृत करने के लिए एसएमएस आधारित एक सुरक्षित सिस्टम बना सकेगा और इस सिस्टम को प्रधानमंत्री की स्वीकृति से लागू कर सकेगा।
.
========राजपत्र अधिसूचना का समाप्ति ========
.
यह राजपत्र अधिसूचना देश की वर्तमान व्यवस्था में किस प्रकार बदलाव लाएगी, यह इस पोस्ट के छठे और सांतवें कमेंट में दर्ज किया गया है। तीन धाराओं के इस ड्राफ्ट के लेखक का यह दावा है कि इस अधिसूचना के राजपत्र में प्रकाशित होने के सिर्फ 4 महीने के भीतर गरीबी का अंत हो जाएगा !!! कैसे ? इन प्रश्नो के उत्तर पांचवें और छठे कमेंट में देखें।
.
यदि आप इस क़ानून का समर्थन करते है तो इस पोस्ट को शेयर करें और अपने सांसद को एसएमएस द्वारा इस पोस्ट का लिंक भेजें
.
